{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"#!/root/miniconda3/bin/python3\\n# eval_repo_cwm.py — Evaluate repo-level CWM (Code World Model / execution simulator) LoRA checkpoint\\n# Input: LoRA checkpoint dir + data/swebench_full/swebench_test.jsonl\\n# Output: per-instance predictions JSONL + summary JSON (accuracy, F1, confusion matrix)\\n# Dependencies: torch, transformers, peft, sklearn, numpy\\n# Key difference from eval_repo_cls.py:\\n#   - CWM generates execution output text, then pass/fail is extracted via keyword matching\\n#   - Longer max_new_tokens (1024 vs 8) since CWM generates execution traces\\n#   - Different system prompt (\\\"execution simulator\\\" vs \\\"code verifier\\\")\\n# Usage:\\n#   python eval_repo_cwm.py --checkpoint_dir &lt;path> --device cuda:1\\n#   python eval_repo_cwm.py --dry_run\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\nimport gc\\nfrom collections import Counter\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom sklearn.metrics import (\\n    accuracy_score, confusion_matrix, f1_score,\\n    precision_score, recall_score,\\n)\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\\\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s\\\")\\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nSYSTEM_PROMPT = (\\n    \\\"You are a code execution simulator. Given a code patch (git diff) and a test name, \\\"\\n    \\\"predict the execution result when the test is run against the patched code.\\\"\\n)\\nUSER_TEMPLATE = \\\"## Patch (git diff)\\\\n{code}\\\\n\\\\n## Test\\\\n{test}\\\\n\\\\nWhat is the execution result?\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef load_jsonl(path):\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        return [json.loads(line) for line in f]\\n\\n\\ndef detect_thinking_support(tokenizer):\\n    try:\\n        tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n            [{\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"hi\\\"}],\\n            tokenize=False, enable_thinking=False,\\n        )\\n        return {\\\"enable_thinking\\\": False}\\n    except TypeError:\\n        return {}\\n\\n\\ndef build_prompt_ids(tokenizer, code, test, tmpl_kw, max_len):\\n    user_msg = USER_TEMPLATE.format(code=code, test=test)\\n    msgs = [\\n        {\\\"role\\\": \\\"system\\\", \\\"content\\\": SYSTEM_PROMPT},\\n        {\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": user_msg},\\n    ]\\n    ids = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n        msgs, tokenize=True, add_generation_prompt=True, **tmpl_kw,\\n    )\\n    if hasattr(ids, \\\"input_ids\\\"):\\n        ids = ids.input_ids\\n    if not isinstance(ids, list):\\n        ids = list(ids)\\n    truncated = len(ids) > max_len\\n    if truncated:\\n        ids = ids[:max_len]\\n    return ids, truncated\\n\\n\\ndef parse_cwm_prediction(text):\\n    if \\\"&lt;/think>\\\" in text:\\n        text = text.split(\\\"&lt;/think>\\\")[-1]\\n    text_lower = text.strip().lower()\\n    if text_lower.startswith(\\\"pass\\\"):\\n        return 1\\n    if text_lower.startswith(\\\"fail\\\"):\\n        return 0\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.43}}"}

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"#!/root/miniconda3/bin/python3\\n# eval_repo_cls.py — Evaluate repo-level CLS (pass/fail verifier) LoRA checkpoint\\n# Input: LoRA checkpoint dir + data/swebench_full/swebench_test.jsonl\\n# Output: per-instance predictions JSONL + summary JSON (accuracy, F1, confusion matrix)\\n# Dependencies: torch, transformers, peft, sklearn, numpy\\n# Usage:\\n#   python eval_repo_cls.py --checkpoint_dir checkpoints_repo_cls_home/checkpoint-600 --device cuda:1\\n#   python eval_repo_cls.py --dry_run  # verify data loading without GPU\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\nimport gc\\nfrom collections import Counter\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom sklearn.metrics import (\\n    accuracy_score, confusion_matrix, f1_score,\\n    precision_score, recall_score,\\n)\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\\\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s\\\")\\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nSYSTEM_PROMPT = (\\n    \\\"You are a code verifier. Given a code patch (git diff) and a test name, \\\"\\n    \\\"predict whether the test will pass or fail when run against the patched code.\\\"\\n)\\nUSER_TEMPLATE = \\\"## Patch (git diff)\\\\n{code}\\\\n\\\\n## Test\\\\n{test}\\\\n\\\\nWill this test pass or fail?\\\"\\nLABEL_MAP = {1: \\\"pass\\\", 0: \\\"fail\\\"}\\n\\n\\ndef load_jsonl(path):\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        return [json.loads(line) for line in f]\\n\\n\\ndef detect_thinking_support(tokenizer):\\n    try:\\n        tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n            [{\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"hi\\\"}],\\n            tokenize=False, enable_thinking=False,\\n        )\\n        return {\\\"enable_thinking\\\": False}\\n    except TypeError:\\n        return {}\\n\\n\\ndef build_prompt_ids(tokenizer, code, test, tmpl_kw, max_len):\\n    user_msg = USER_TEMPLATE.format(code=code, test=test)\\n    msgs = [\\n        {\\\"role\\\": \\\"system\\\", \\\"content\\\": SYSTEM_PROMPT},\\n        {\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": user_msg},\\n    ]\\n    ids = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n        msgs, tokenize=True, add_generation_prompt=True, **tmpl_kw,\\n    )\\n    if hasattr(ids, \\\"input_ids\\\"):\\n        ids = ids.input_ids\\n    if not isinstance(ids, list):\\n        ids = list(ids)\\n    truncated = len(ids) > max_len\\n    if truncated:\\n        ids = ids[:max_len]\\n    return ids, truncated\\n\\n\\ndef parse_prediction(text):\\n    if \\\"&lt;/think>\\\" in text:\\n        text = text.split(\\\"&lt;/think>\\\")[-1]\\n    text = text.strip().lower()\\n    if text.startswith(\\\"pass\\\"):\\n        return 1\\n    if text.startswith(\\\"fail\\\"):\\n        return 0\\n    if \\\"pass\\\" in text and \\\"fail\\\" not in text:\\n        return 1\\n    if \\\"fail\\\" in text and \\\"pass\\\" not in text:\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.52}}"
    }
  }
}

Given a code patch (git diff) and a test name, \\\"\\n    \\\"predict the execution result when the test is run against the patched code.\\\"\\n)\\nUSER_TEMPLATE = \\\"## Patch (git diff)\\\\n{code}\\\\n\\\\n## Test\\\\n{test}\\\\n\\\\nWhat is the execution result?\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef load_jsonl(path):\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        return [json.loads(line) for line in f]\\n\\n\\ndef detect_thinking_support(tokenizer):\\n    try:\\n        tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n            [{\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"hi\\\"}],\\n            tokenize=False, enable_thinking=False,\\n        )\\n        return {\\\"enable_thinking\\\": False}\\n    except TypeError:\\n        return {}\\n\\n\\ndef build_prompt_ids(tokenizer, code, test, tmpl_kw, max_len):\\n    user_msg = USER_TEMPLATE.format(code=code, test=test)\\n    msgs = [\\n        {\\\"role\\\": \\\"system\\\", \\\"content\\\": SYSTEM_PROMPT},\\n        {\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": user_msg},\\n    ]\\n    ids = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n        msgs, tokenize=True, add_generation_prompt=True, **tmpl_kw,\\n    )\\n    if hasattr(ids, \\\"input_ids\\\"):\\n        ids = ids.input_ids\\n    if not isinstance(ids, list):\\n        ids = list(ids)\\n    truncated = len(ids) > max_len\\n    if truncated:\\n        ids = ids[:max_len]\\n    return ids, truncated\\n\\n\\ndef parse_cwm_prediction(text):\\n    if \\\"&lt;/think>\\\" in text:\\n        text = text.split(\\\"&lt;/think>\\\")[-1]\\n    text_lower = text.strip().lower()\\n    if text_lower.startswith(\\\"pass\\\"):\\n        return 1\\n    if text_lower.startswith(\\\"fail\\\"):\\n        return 0\\n    fail_kw = [\\\"error\\\", \\\"assert\\\", \\\"traceback\\\", \\\"exception\\\", \\\"fail\\\"]\\n    pass_kw = [\\\"pass\\\", \\\"success\\\", \\\"completed\\\"]\\n    has_fail = any(p in text_lower for p in fail_kw)\\n    has_pass = any(p in text_lower for p in pass_kw)\\n    if has_fail and not has_pass:\\n        return 0\\n    if has_pass and not has_fail:\\n        return 1\\n    return -1\\n\\n\\ndef load_model(checkpoint_dir, base_model_name, device):\\n    from peft import PeftModel\\n    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\n\\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(\\n        checkpoint_dir, trust_remote_code=True, padding_side=\\\"left\\\",\\n    )\\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\\n\\n    try:\\n        import flash_attn\\n        attn_impl = \\\"flash_attention_2\\\"\\n    except ImportError:\\n        attn_impl = \\\"sdpa\\\"\\n    log.info(f\\\"Attention: {attn_impl}\\\")\\n\\n    base_model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\n        base_model_name,\\n        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\\n        trust_remote_code=True,\\n        attn_implementation=attn_impl,\\n    )\\n    model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(base_model, checkpoint_dir)\\n    model.eval()\\n    model = model.to(device)\\n    return model, tokenizer\\n\\n\\ndef run_inference(model, tokenizer, test_data, batch_size, max_seq_length,\\n                  max_new_tokens, device):\\n    tmpl_kw = detect_thinking_support(tokenizer)\\n    total = len(test_data)\\n    label_counts = Counter(ex[\\\"label\\\"] for ex in test_data)\\n    majority_class = 1 if label_counts.get(1, 0) >= label_counts.get(0, 0) else 0\\n\\n    results = []\\n    n_unparseable = 0\\n\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": Given a code patch (git diff) and a test name, \\\"\\n    \\\"predict the execution result when the test is run against the patched code.\\\"\\n)\\nUSER_TEMPLATE = \\\"## Patch (git diff)\\\\n{code}\\\\n\\\\n## Test\\\\n{test}\\\\n\\\\nWhat is the execution result?\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef load_jsonl(path):\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        return [json.loads(line) for line in f]\\n\\n\\ndef detect_thinking_support(tokenizer):\\n    try:\\n        tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n            [{\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"hi\\\"}],\\n            tokenize=False, enable_thinking=False,\\n        )\\n        return {\\\"enable_thinking\\\": False}\\n    except TypeError:\\n        return {}\\n\\n\\ndef build_prompt_ids(tokenizer, code, test, tmpl_kw, max_len):\\n    user_msg = USER_TEMPLATE.format(code=code, test=test)\\n    msgs = [\\n        {\\\"role\\\": \\\"system\\\", \\\"content\\\": SYSTEM_PROMPT},\\n        {\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": user_msg},\\n    ]\\n    ids = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n        msgs, tokenize=True, add_generation_prompt=True, **tmpl_kw,\\n    )\\n    if hasattr(ids, Given a code patch (git diff) and a test name, \\\"\\n    \\\"predict the execution result when the test is run against the patched code.\\\"\\n)\\nUSER_TEMPLATE = \\\"## Patch (git diff)\\\\n{code}\\\\n\\\\n## Test\\\\n{test}\\\\n\\\\nWhat is the execution result?\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef load_jsonl(path):\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        return [json.loads(line) for line in f]\\n\\n\\ndef detect_thinking_support(tokenizer):\\n    try:\\n        tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n            [{\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"hi\\\"}],\\n            tokenize=False, enable_thinking=False,\\n        )\\n        return {\\\"enable_thinking\\\": False}\\n    except TypeError:\\n        return {}\\n\\n\\ndef build_prompt_ids(tokenizer, code, test, tmpl_kw, max_len):\\n    user_msg = USER_TEMPLATE.format(code=code, test=test)\\n    msgs = [\\n        {\\\"role\\\": \\\"system\\\", \\\"content\\\": SYSTEM_PROMPT},\\n        {\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": user_msg},\\n    ]\\n    ids = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n        msgs, tokenize=True, add_generation_prompt=True, **tmpl_kw,\\n    )\\n    if hasattr(ids, \\\"input_ids\\\"):\\n        ids = ids.input_ids\\n    if not isinstance(ids, list):\\n        ids = list(ids)\\n    truncated = len(ids) > max_len\\n    if truncated:\\n        ids = base_model_name, device):\\n    from peft import PeftModel\\n    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\n\\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(\\n
